"From there, the actor performed discovery from inside the network using different RATs in combination with other tools, for example, to harvest credentials or proxy connections," Yun Zheng Hu and Mick Koomen said . The vulnerability, CVE-2025-55177  (CVSS score: 5.4), relates to a case of insufficient authorization of linked device synchronization messages. Internal researchers on the WhatsApp Security Team have been credited with discovering and rerating the bug.   The Meta-owned company said  the issue "could have allowed an unrelated user to trigger processing of content from an arbitrary URL on a target's device."
